We propose the Contraction Mapping Model (CMM), a novel architecture that reformulates discrete recursive reasoning into continuous Neural Ordinary and Stochastic Differential Equations (NODEs/NSDEs). By explicitly enforcing the convergence of the latent phase point to a stable equilibrium state and mitigating feature collapse with a hyperspherical repulsion loss, the CMM provides a mathematically grounded and highly stable reasoning engine. On the Sudoku-Extreme benchmark, a 5M-parameter CMM achieves a state-of-the-art accuracy of 93.7 %, outperforming the 27M-parameter HRM (55.0 %) and 5M-parameter TRM (87.4 %). Remarkably, even when aggressively compressed to an ultra-tiny footprint of just 0.26M parameters, the CMM retains robust predictive power, achieving 85.4 % on Sudoku-Extreme and 82.2 % on the Maze benchmark. These results establish a new frontier for extreme parameter efficiency, proving that mathematically rigorous latent dynamics can effectively replace brute-force scaling in artificial reasoning.

</details>


### [187] [Ran Score: a LLM-based Evaluation Score for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22935)
*Ran Zhang,Yucong Lin,Zhaoli Su,Bowen Liu,Danni Ai,Tianyu Fu,Deqiang Xiao,Jingfan Fan,Yuanyuan Wang,Mingwei Gao,Yuwan Hu,Shuya Gao,Jingtao Li,Jian Yang,Hong Song,Hongliang Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个结合临床医生专业知识和大语言模型的框架，用于从胸部X光报告中提取多标签发现，并定义了Ran Score用于报告评估，显著提升了罕见异常检测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前胸部X光报告生成和自动评估存在两个主要问题：1) 对低患病率异常的识别能力差；2) 对临床重要语言（如否定和模糊表达）的处理不足。这限制了报告评估的准确性和临床实用性。

Method: 开发了一个临床医生引导的框架，结合人类专业知识和大语言模型，从自由文本胸部X光报告中提取多标签发现。使用该方法定义了Ran Score，这是一个发现级别的报告评估指标。在三个非重叠的MIMIC-CXR-EN队列和一个独立的ChestX-CN验证队列上进行实验，优化提示词，建立放射科医生参考标签，并评估报告生成模型。

Result: 优化后的框架将MIMIC-CXR-EN开发队列的宏观平均得分从0.753提升到0.956。在直接可比较的标签上，超过CheXbert基准15.7个百分点。在ChestX-CN验证队列上显示出稳健的泛化能力。临床医生引导的提示优化显著提高了与放射科医生参考标准的一致性。

Conclusion: 临床医生引导的提示优化能够显著改善与放射科医生参考标准的一致性。Ran Score实现了发现级别的报告保真度评估，特别是在低患病率异常检测方面表现出色，为胸部X光报告生成和评估提供了更准确、临床相关的工具。

Abstract: Chest X-ray report generation and automated evaluation are limited by poor recognition of low-prevalence abnormalities and inadequate handling of clinically important language, including negation and ambiguity. We develop a clinician-guided framework combining human expertise and large language models for multi-label finding extraction from free-text chest X-ray reports and use it to define Ran Score, a finding-level metric for report evaluation. Using three non-overlapping MIMIC-CXR-EN cohorts from a public chest X-ray dataset and an independent ChestX-CN validation cohort, we optimize prompts, establish radiologist-derived reference labels and evaluate report generation models. The optimized framework improves the macro-averaged score from 0.753 to 0.956 on the MIMIC-CXR-EN development cohort, exceeds the CheXbert benchmark by 15.7 percentage points on directly comparable labels, and shows robust generalization on the ChestX-CN validation cohort. Here we show that clinician-guided prompt optimization improves agreement with a radiologist-derived reference standard and that Ran Score enables finding-level evaluation of report fidelity, particularly for low-prevalence abnormalities.

</details>


### [188] [Optimizing Small Language Models for NL2SQL via Chain-of-Thought Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22942)
*Anshul Solanki,Sanchit Latawa,Koushik Chakraborty,Navneet Kamboj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现微调小型语言模型在NL2SQL任务上比微调大型模型更有效，通过CoT推理增强数据集可将小型模型准确率从36%提升至54.5%，实现成本效益平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大型语言模型在NL2SQL任务上表现出色，但其高昂的推理成本限制了企业级规模化部署，需要探索更经济高效的解决方案

Method: 对比微调大型模型(Gemini 2.5 Flash/Lite)和小型模型(Qwen)在NL2SQL任务上的表现，通过标准数据集和增强的Chain-of-Thought推理数据集进行训练

Result: 微调大型模型效果有限且容易过拟合复杂查询，而小型模型从36%提升至45%，加入CoT推理后达到54.5%准确率，虽低于大型模型但满足业务关键性能阈值

Conclusion: 通过转移推理模式，计算效率更高的小型模型可以接近生产级性能，在显著降低成本、降低推理延迟的同时满足业务准确性要求

Abstract: Translating Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) remains a critical bottleneck for democratization of data in enterprises. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) like Gemini 2.5 and other LLMs have demonstrated impressive zero-shot capabilities, their high inference costs limit deployment at scale. This paper explores the efficacy of fine-tuning both large and small language models on NL2SQL tasks.
  Our research reveals a counter-intuitive scaling phenomenon. Fine-tuning large models (Gemini 2.5 Flash/Lite) on standard datasets yields negligible returns, often leading to overfitting on complex queries. Conversely, small models (Qwen) show significant gains. Fine-tuning improved the small model baseline from 36% to 45%, and further enriching the dataset with explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning surged accuracy to 54.5%(Fig 2). While this is still lower than the accuracy of large models like Gemini 2.5 , it does serve the business goal of significant cost reduction, latency in inference time and also meeting the business critical performance accuracy threshold.This paper demonstrates that transferring reasoning patterns enables compute-efficient smaller models to approach production-grade performance.

</details>


### [189] [PersonalQ: Select, Quantize, and Serve Personalized Diffusion Models for Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22943)
*Qirui Wang,Qi Guo,Yiding Sun,Junkai Yang,Dongxu Zhang,Shanmin Pang,Qing Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PersonalQ：一个统一框架，通过共享触发器令牌信号连接检查点选择和量化，实现个性化文本到图像生成的高效服务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化文本到图像生成需要微调扩散模型为概念特定的检查点，但高效服务这些检查点面临两大挑战：自然语言请求的歧义性可能导致错误路由到视觉相似的检查点；标准后训练量化可能破坏编码个性化概念的脆弱表示

Method: 提出PersonalQ统一框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Check-in：通过意图感知混合检索和基于LLM的重新排序实现意图对齐选择，在多个意图合理时询问澄清问题，然后插入选定检查点的规范触发器重写提示；2) 触发器感知量化(TAQ)：在交叉注意力中应用触发器感知混合精度，保留触发器条件键/值行及其注意力权重，同时积极量化其余路径以实现内存高效推理

Result: 实验表明，PersonalQ在意图对齐方面优于检索和重新排序基线，而TAQ相比先前的扩散PTQ方法提供了更强的压缩-质量权衡，能够在保持保真度的同时实现个性化检查点的可扩展服务

Conclusion: PersonalQ通过共享触发器令牌信号统一检查点选择和量化，解决了个性化文本到图像生成中的意图对齐和量化失真问题，实现了高效且保真的个性化检查点服务

Abstract: Personalized text-to-image generation lets users fine-tune diffusion models into repositories of concept-specific checkpoints, but serving these repositories efficiently is difficult for two reasons: natural-language requests are often ambiguous and can be misrouted to visually similar checkpoints, and standard post-training quantization can distort the fragile representations that encode personalized concepts. We present PersonalQ, a unified framework that connects checkpoint selection and quantization through a shared signal -- the checkpoint's trigger token. Check-in performs intent-aligned selection by combining intent-aware hybrid retrieval with LLM-based reranking over checkpoint context and asks a brief clarification question only when multiple intents remain plausible; it then rewrites the prompt by inserting the selected checkpoint's canonical trigger. Complementing this, Trigger-Aware Quantization (TAQ) applies trigger-aware mixed precision in cross-attention, preserving trigger-conditioned key/value rows (and their attention weights) while aggressively quantizing the remaining pathways for memory-efficient inference. Experiments show that PersonalQ improves intent alignment over retrieval and reranking baselines, while TAQ consistently offers a stronger compression-quality trade-off than prior diffusion PTQ methods, enabling scalable serving of personalized checkpoints without sacrificing fidelity.

</details>


### [190] [Where Experts Disagree, Models Fail: Detecting Implicit Legal Citations in French Court Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22973)
*Avrile Floro,Tamara Dhorasoo,Soline Pellez,Nils Holzenberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文开发了检测法国法院判决中隐含引用民法典的方法，通过专家标注基准数据集评估模型性能，发现专家分歧预测模型失败，并提出了改进的排名方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是量化法院在多大程度上隐含地适用法定规则，这需要区分法律推理与语义相似性。具体关注法国一审法院判决中对民法典的隐含引用。

Method: 创建了1,015个段落-条款对的专家标注基准数据集，采用监督集成方法（F1=0.70），并将任务重构为top-k排名，利用多模型共识进行无监督分析。

Result: 专家间一致性中等（κ=0.33），43%的分歧涉及事实描述与法律推理的边界。68%的假阳性出现在33%的专家分歧案例中。无监督top-k排名在k=200时达到76%的精确率，剩余假阳性往往揭示法律模糊应用而非明显错误。

Conclusion: 研究表明，专家分歧能有效预测模型失败，将任务重构为排名并利用多模型共识可在无监督设置下获得良好性能，且假阳性往往反映法律应用的模糊性而非技术错误。

Abstract: Computational methods applied to legal scholarship hold the promise of analyzing law at scale. We start from a simple question: how often do courts implicitly apply statutory rules? This requires distinguishing legal reasoning from semantic similarity. We focus on implicit citation of the French Civil Code in first-instance court decisions and introduce a benchmark of 1,015 passage-article pairs annotated by three legal experts. We show that expert disagreement predicts model failures. Inter-annotator agreement is moderate ($κ$ = 0.33) with 43% of disagreements involving the boundary between factual description and legal reasoning. Our supervised ensemble achieves F1 = 0.70 (77% accuracy), but this figure conceals an asymmetry: 68% of false positives fall on the 33% of cases where the annotators disagreed. Despite these limits, reframing the task as top-k ranking and leveraging multi-model consensus yields 76% precision at k = 200 in an unsupervised setting. Moreover, the remaining false positives tend to surface legally ambiguous applications rather than obvious errors.

</details>


### [191] [JFTA-Bench: Evaluate LLM's Ability of Tracking and Analyzing Malfunctions Using Fault Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22978)
*Yuhui Wang,Zhixiong Yang,Ming Zhang,Shihan Dou,Zhiheng Xi,Enyu Zhou,Senjie Jin,Yujiong Shen,Dingwei Zhu,Yi Dong,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出故障树的文本表示方法，构建多轮对话基准评估模型在复杂环境中的故障定位能力，Gemini 2.5 pro表现最佳


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有故障树以图像形式存储，无法直接被大语言模型处理，限制了其在故障跟踪和分析中的应用。需要一种文本表示方法使故障树能被LLM直接处理，从而辅助故障定位。

Method: 1. 提出故障树的新型文本表示方法；2. 构建包含3130个条目、平均40.75轮对话的多轮对话基准，强调复杂环境中的鲁棒交互；3. 训练端到端模型生成模糊信息模拟用户行为；4. 引入长程回滚和恢复流程模拟用户错误场景。

Result: 构建了包含3130个条目的基准数据集，平均每个条目40.75轮对话。通过评估模型在故障定位、任务跟踪和错误恢复方面的综合能力，发现Gemini 2.5 pro在所有测试模型中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 提出的故障树文本表示方法使故障树能被LLM直接处理，构建的基准能有效评估模型在复杂故障定位场景中的综合能力，为故障诊断辅助系统的发展提供了重要基础。

Abstract: In the maintenance of complex systems, fault trees are used to locate problems and provide targeted solutions. To enable fault trees stored as images to be directly processed by large language models, which can assist in tracking and analyzing malfunctions, we propose a novel textual representation of fault trees. Building on it, we construct a benchmark for multi-turn dialogue systems that emphasizes robust interaction in complex environments, evaluating a model's ability to assist in malfunction localization, which contains $3130$ entries and $40.75$ turns per entry on average. We train an end-to-end model to generate vague information to reflect user behavior and introduce long-range rollback and recovery procedures to simulate user error scenarios, enabling assessment of a model's integrated capabilities in task tracking and error recovery, and Gemini 2.5 pro archives the best performance.

</details>


### [192] [On the use of Aggregation Operators to improve Human Identification using Dental Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23003)
*Antonio D. Villegas-Yeguas,Guillermo R-García,Tzipi Kahana,Jorge Pinares Toledo,Esi Sharon,Oscar Ibañez,Oscar Cordón*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了可解释的自动牙科记录比对聚合方法，使用数据驱动的词典序、模糊逻辑和机器学习技术，显著提升了现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前法医牙科学中的牙科记录自动比对方法要么过于简单未能充分利用比对信息，要么缺乏同行评审导致内部机制不透明。需要设计既高效又可被专家理解和验证的聚合机制。

Method: 提出了三种聚合方法：1) 基于数据驱动的词典序聚合；2) 已知的模糊逻辑聚合方法；3) 机器学习技术作为聚合机制。使用基于七种不同标准的最新编码，并在来自两个不同人群的215个法医案例上进行验证。

Result: 白盒机器学习技术作为聚合模型取得了最佳性能，平均排名从2.02到2.21，显著优于现有方法（平均排名3.91），同时保持了方法的可解释性和可理解性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，使用白盒机器学习技术作为聚合模型可以在不损害方法可解释性的前提下，显著提升牙科记录自动比对的性能，为法医牙科学提供更可靠和透明的工具。

Abstract: The comparison of dental records is a standardized technique in forensic dentistry used to speed up the identification of individuals in multiple-comparison scenarios. Specifically, the odontogram comparison is a procedure to compute criteria that will be used to perform a ranking. State-of-the-art automatic methods either make use of simple techniques, without utilizing the full potential of the information obtained from a comparison, or their internal behavior is not known due to the lack of peer-reviewed publications. This work aims to design aggregation mechanisms to automatically compare pairs of dental records that can be understood and validated by experts, improving the current methods. To do so, we introduce different aggregation approaches using the state-of-the-art codification, based on seven different criteria. In particular, we study the performance of i) data-driven lexicographical order-based aggregations, ii) well-known fuzzy logic aggregation methods and iii) machine learning techniques as aggregation mechanisms. To validate our proposals, 215 forensic cases from two different populations have been used. The results obtained show how the use of white-box machine learning techniques as aggregation models (average ranking from 2.02 to 2.21) are able to improve the state-of-the-art (average ranking of 3.91) without compromising the explainability and interpretability of the method.

</details>


### [193] [Can Large Language Models Reason and Optimize Under Constraints?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23004)
*Fabien Bernier,Salah Ghamizi,Pantelis Dogoulis,Maxime Cordy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs在约束优化问题（特别是最优潮流问题）上的能力评估显示存在显著缺陷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在多种自然语言任务上表现出色，但在抽象推理和约束优化问题上的能力尚未充分探索，特别是在电力系统优化等现实世界约束问题中

Method: 引入一个具有挑战性的评估框架，测试LLMs在最优潮流问题中所需的基本技能：推理、结构化输入处理、算术和约束优化

Result: 最先进的LLMs在大多数任务中失败，推理型LLMs在最复杂场景中仍然失败，揭示了LLMs在约束下进行结构化推理的关键能力缺口

Conclusion: LLMs处理约束优化问题的能力存在严重不足，需要开发更强大的LLM助手来应对现实世界的电网优化挑战，本文提供了一个严格的测试环境

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated great capabilities across diverse natural language tasks; yet their ability to solve abstraction and optimization problems with constraints remains scarcely explored. In this paper, we investigate whether LLMs can reason and optimize under the physical and operational constraints of Optimal Power Flow (OPF) problem. We introduce a challenging evaluation setup that requires a set of fundamental skills such as reasoning, structured input handling, arithmetic, and constrained optimization. Our evaluation reveals that SoTA LLMs fail in most of the tasks, and that reasoning LLMs still fail in the most complex settings. Our findings highlight critical gaps in LLMs' ability to handle structured reasoning under constraints, and this work provides a rigorous testing environment for developing more capable LLM assistants that can tackle real-world power grid optimization problems.

</details>


### [194] [Minibal: Balanced Game-Playing Without Opponent Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23059)
*Quentin Cohen-Solal,Tristan Cazenave*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Minibal算法：专为平衡游戏设计的Minimax变体，旨在让AI既能挑战人类玩家又不会过度压制，提升人机交互的娱乐性和教育价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏AI（如AlphaZero、Athénan）虽然强大但过于压制人类玩家，导致游戏体验差、教育价值有限。需要解决平衡游戏问题，让AI既能挑战对手又不会过度主导或故意让步。

Method: 提出Minibal（Minimize & Balance），这是专门为平衡游戏设计的Minimax变体。基于此概念，提出了Unbounded Minimax算法的多种修改版本，明确旨在发现平衡策略。

Result: 在七种棋盘游戏上的实验表明，其中一个变体始终能实现最平衡的游戏体验，平均结果接近完美平衡。

Conclusion: Minibal为设计既具挑战性又具吸引力的AI智能体提供了有前景的基础，适用于娱乐和严肃游戏场景。

Abstract: Recent advances in game AI, such as AlphaZero and Athénan, have achieved superhuman performance across a wide range of board games. While highly powerful, these agents are ill-suited for human-AI interaction, as they consistently overwhelm human players, offering little enjoyment and limited educational value. This paper addresses the problem of balanced play, in which an agent challenges its opponent without either dominating or conceding.
  We introduce Minibal (Minimize & Balance), a variant of Minimax specifically designed for balanced play. Building on this concept, we propose several modifications of the Unbounded Minimax algorithm explicitly aimed at discovering balanced strategies.
  Experiments conducted across seven board games demonstrate that one variant consistently achieves the most balanced play, with average outcomes close to perfect balance. These results establish Minibal as a promising foundation for designing AI agents that are both challenging and engaging, suitable for both entertainment and serious games.

</details>


### [195] [MedCausalX: Adaptive Causal Reasoning with Self-Reflection for Trustworthy Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23085)
*Jianxin Lin,Chunzheng Zhu,Peter J. Kneuertz,Yunfei Bai,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedCausalX：一个端到端框架，通过在医学视觉语言模型中显式建模因果推理链，解决现有医学链式思维模型缺乏因果推理机制的问题，提升临床可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学链式思维模型缺乏显式的因果推理机制，容易受到虚假相关性的影响，限制了临床可靠性。需要解决三个核心挑战：自适应触发因果校正、构建高质量因果-虚假对比样本、保持跨推理轨迹的因果一致性。

Method: 提出MedCausalX框架：1）引入CRMed数据集，提供细粒度解剖标注、结构化因果推理链和反事实变体；2）采用两阶段自适应反思架构，配备<因果>和<验证>标记，使模型能自主决定何时及如何进行因果分析和验证；3）通过错误归因强化学习优化的轨迹级因果校正目标，精炼推理链以区分真实因果依赖与捷径关联。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，MedCausalX始终优于最先进方法：诊断一致性提升+5.4分，幻觉减少超过10分，获得顶级空间定位IoU，为因果基础的医学推理设定了新标准。

Conclusion: MedCausalX通过显式建模因果推理链，有效解决了医学视觉语言模型中因果推理的挑战，显著提升了诊断可靠性和临床实用性，为可解释医学诊断提供了新的框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled interpretable medical diagnosis by integrating visual perception with linguistic reasoning. Yet, existing medical chain-of-thought (CoT) models lack explicit mechanisms to represent and enforce causal reasoning, leaving them vulnerable to spurious correlations and limiting their clinical reliability. We pinpoint three core challenges in medical CoT reasoning: how to adaptively trigger causal correction, construct high-quality causal-spurious contrastive samples, and maintain causal consistency across reasoning trajectories. To address these challenges, we propose MedCausalX, an end-to-end framework explicitly models causal reasoning chains in medical VLMs. We first introduce the CRMed dataset providing fine-grained anatomical annotations, structured causal reasoning chains, and counterfactual variants that guide the learning of causal relationships beyond superficial correlations. Building upon CRMed, MedCausalX employs a two-stage adaptive reflection architecture equipped with $\langle$causal$\rangle$ and $\langle$verify$\rangle$ tokens, enabling the model to autonomously determine when and how to perform causal analysis and verification. Finally, a trajectory-level causal correction objective optimized through error-attributed reinforcement learning refines the reasoning chain, allowing the model to distinguish genuine causal dependencies from shortcut associations. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks show that MedCausalX consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, improving diagnostic consistency by +5.4 points, reducing hallucination by over 10 points, and attaining top spatial grounding IoU, thereby setting a new standard for causally grounded medical reasoning.

</details>


### [196] [Between Rules and Reality: On the Context Sensitivity of LLM Moral Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23114)
*Adrian Sauter,Mona Schirmer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文引入Contextual MoralChoice数据集，研究LLM在道德困境中的情境敏感性，发现模型与人类对情境变化的反应不同，并提出激活引导方法控制模型的情境敏感性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM道德研究主要关注固定场景，而人类道德决策高度依赖情境。需要研究LLM在系统情境变化下的道德判断，填补这一研究空白

Method: 1. 创建Contextual MoralChoice数据集，包含基于道德心理学已知会影响人类判断的三种系统情境变化：结果主义、情感和关系情境
2. 评估22个LLM在情境变化下的道德判断
3. 与人类调查结果比较模型与人类的情境敏感性差异
4. 提出激活引导方法控制模型的情境敏感性

Result: 1. 几乎所有模型都表现出情境敏感性，倾向于违反规则的行为
2. 模型与人类对情境变化的触发因素不同
3. 在基础情况下与人类判断对齐的模型，在其情境敏感性方面不一定与人类对齐
4. 激活引导方法能够可靠地增加或减少模型的情境敏感性

Conclusion: LLM在道德判断中表现出显著的情境敏感性，但这种敏感性与人类存在差异。激活引导方法为控制模型的情境敏感性提供了有效途径，对开发更符合人类道德判断的AI系统具有重要意义

Abstract: A human's moral decision depends heavily on the context. Yet research on LLM morality has largely studied fixed scenarios. We address this gap by introducing Contextual MoralChoice, a dataset of moral dilemmas with systematic contextual variations known from moral psychology to shift human judgment: consequentialist, emotional, and relational. Evaluating 22 LLMs, we find that nearly all models are context-sensitive, shifting their judgments toward rule-violating behavior. Comparing with a human survey, we find that models and humans are most triggered by different contextual variations, and that a model aligned with human judgments in the base case is not necessarily aligned in its contextual sensitivity. This raises the question of controlling contextual sensitivity, which we address with an activation steering approach that can reliably increase or decrease a model's contextual sensitivity.

</details>


### [197] [Describe-Then-Act: Proactive Agent Steering via Distilled Language-Action World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23149)
*Massimiliano Pappa,Luca Romani,Valentino Sacco,Alessio Palma,Stéphane Lathuilière,Fabio Galasso,Xavier Alameda-Pineda,Indro Spinelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DILLO是一种快速转向层，通过跨模态蒸馏训练，利用策略的潜在状态和计划动作来预测语义结果，绕过视觉生成，实现14倍加速，提高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键型智能体部署需要预知行动后果，但当前基于视觉仿真的世界模型延迟过高（每步数秒）。本文挑战视觉处理对故障预防的必要性假设，认为训练策略的潜在状态结合计划动作已包含足够信息来预测行动结果。

Method: 提出DILLO（蒸馏语言-动作世界模型），通过跨模态蒸馏训练：特权视觉语言模型教师标注离线轨迹，潜在条件大型语言模型学生学习预测语义结果。创建纯文本推理路径，完全绕过繁重的视觉生成。

Result: DILLO实现14倍于基线的加速，在MetaWorld和LIBERO实验中能生成高保真的下一状态描述，并能引导策略，将情节成功率平均提高9.3个百分点，最高提升15个百分点。

Conclusion: 视觉仿真对故障预防并非必要，策略的潜在状态和计划动作已编码足够信息来预测行动结果。DILLO通过"描述-行动"范式替代"仿真-行动"范式，提供快速、高效的故障预防方案。

Abstract: Deploying safety-critical agents requires anticipating the consequences of actions before they are executed. While world models offer a paradigm for this proactive foresight, current approaches relying on visual simulation incur prohibitive latencies, often exceeding several seconds per step. In this work, we challenge the assumption that visual processing is necessary for failure prevention. We show that a trained policy's latent state, combined with its planned actions, already encodes sufficient information to anticipate action outcomes, making visual simulation redundant for failure prevention. To this end, we introduce DILLO (DIstiLLed Language-ActiOn World Model), a fast steering layer that shifts the paradigm from "simulate-then-act" to "describe-then-act." DILLO is trained via cross-modal distillation, where a privileged Vision Language Model teacher annotates offline trajectories and a latent-conditioned Large Language Model student learns to predict semantic outcomes. This creates a text-only inference path, bypassing heavy visual generation entirely, achieving a 14x speedup over baselines. Experiments on MetaWorld and LIBERO demonstrate that DILLO produces high-fidelity descriptions of the next state and is able to steer the policy, improving episode success rate by up to 15 pp and 9.3 pp on average across tasks.

</details>


### [198] [PERMA: Benchmarking Personalized Memory Agents via Event-Driven Preference and Realistic Task Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23231)
*Shuochen Liu,Junyi Zhu,Long Shu,Junda Lin,Yuhao Chen,Haotian Zhang,Chao Zhang,Derong Xu,Jia Li,Bo Tang,Zhiyu Li,Feiyu Xiong,Enhong Chen,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PERMA是一个评估大语言模型长期记忆能力的基准，专注于随时间推移保持角色一致性，超越静态偏好回忆，模拟真实世界个性化场景中的渐进偏好演变和噪声环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法通常将偏好相关对话与无关对话交织，将其简化为"大海捞针"式检索，忽略了驱动用户偏好演变的事件间关系。这些设置忽视了真实世界个性化的基本特征：偏好是逐渐出现并在噪声环境中跨交互累积的。

Method: 引入PERMA基准，包含时间顺序的交互事件，跨越多个会话和领域，随时间插入偏好相关查询。设计了多项选择和交互式任务来探测模型在交互时间线上对角色理解的能力。基准还整合了文本变异性和语言对齐来模拟真实世界数据中的不稳定用户输入和个人语言习惯。

Result: 实验表明，通过链接相关交互，先进记忆系统能够提取更精确的偏好并减少token消耗，优于传统的原始对话语义检索。然而，这些系统仍然难以在时间深度和跨领域干扰下保持连贯的角色，突显了智能体中需要更强大的个性化记忆管理。

Conclusion: PERMA基准揭示了当前记忆系统在长期角色一致性方面的局限性，强调了开发更健壮的个性化记忆管理方法的必要性，以构建能够适应用户不断变化需求的智能体。

Abstract: Empowering large language models with long-term memory is crucial for building agents that adapt to users' evolving needs. However, prior evaluations typically interleave preference-related dialogues with irrelevant conversations, reducing the task to needle-in-a-haystack retrieval while ignoring relationships between events that drive the evolution of user preferences. Such settings overlook a fundamental characteristic of real-world personalization: preferences emerge gradually and accumulate across interactions within noisy contexts. To bridge this gap, we introduce PERMA, a benchmark designed to evaluate persona consistency over time beyond static preference recall. Additionally, we incorporate (1) text variability and (2) linguistic alignment to simulate erratic user inputs and individual idiolects in real-world data. PERMA consists of temporally ordered interaction events spanning multiple sessions and domains, with preference-related queries inserted over time. We design both multiple-choice and interactive tasks to probe the model's understanding of persona along the interaction timeline. Experiments demonstrate that by linking related interactions, advanced memory systems can extract more precise preferences and reduce token consumption, outperforming traditional semantic retrieval of raw dialogues. Nevertheless, they still struggle to maintain a coherent persona across temporal depth and cross-domain interference, highlighting the need for more robust personalized memory management in agents. Our code and data are open-sourced at https://github.com/PolarisLiu1/PERMA.

</details>


### [199] [MemCollab: Cross-Agent Memory Collaboration via Contrastive Trajectory Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23234)
*Yurui Chang,Yiran Wu,Qingyun Wu,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemCollab是一个协作式记忆框架，通过对比不同智能体在同一任务上的推理轨迹来构建智能体无关的记忆，从而在异构智能体之间共享记忆系统，提高推理准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的记忆机制通常是针对单个智能体设计的，将存储的知识与特定模型的推理风格紧密耦合。在异构智能体部署中，需要探索能否在不同模型之间共享单一记忆系统。研究发现，简单地在智能体之间转移记忆会降低性能，因为这种记忆将任务相关知识与智能体特定偏见纠缠在一起。

Method: 提出MemCollab框架：1）通过对比不同智能体在同一任务上的推理轨迹来构建智能体无关的记忆；2）对比过程提炼出抽象推理约束，捕捉共享的任务级不变性，同时抑制智能体特定的伪影；3）引入任务感知检索机制，根据任务类别条件化记忆访问，确保在推理时只使用相关约束。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准测试上的实验表明，MemCollab能够一致地提高不同智能体的准确性和推理时间效率，包括跨模态家族设置。协作构建的记忆可以作为多样化LLM智能体的共享推理资源。

Conclusion: MemCollab框架成功解决了异构智能体之间共享记忆的挑战，通过对比推理轨迹构建智能体无关的记忆，有效分离任务知识与智能体偏见，为多样化LLM智能体提供了可共享的推理资源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents rely on memory mechanisms to reuse knowledge from past problem-solving experiences. Existing approaches typically construct memory in a per-agent manner, tightly coupling stored knowledge to a single model's reasoning style. In modern deployments with heterogeneous agents, a natural question arises: can a single memory system be shared across different models? We found that naively transferring memory between agents often degrades performance, as such memory entangles task-relevant knowledge with agent-specific biases. To address this challenge, we propose MemCollab, a collaborative memory framework that constructs agent-agnostic memory by contrasting reasoning trajectories generated by different agents on the same task. This contrastive process distills abstract reasoning constraints that capture shared task-level invariants while suppressing agent-specific artifacts. We further introduce a task-aware retrieval mechanism that conditions memory access on task category, ensuring that only relevant constraints are used at inference time. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks demonstrate that MemCollab consistently improves both accuracy and inference-time efficiency across diverse agents, including cross-modal-family settings. Our results show that the collaboratively constructed memory can function as a shared reasoning resource for diverse LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [200] [Online library learning in human visual puzzle solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23244)
*Pinzhe Zhao,Emanuele Sansone,Marta Kryven,Bonan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 人类在视觉谜题任务中通过创建和重用"助手"（中间构造）来学习复杂任务，随着经验增加，使用变得更加高效和有选择性，计算模型显示人类决策时间与程序归纳模型的搜索空间相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人类如何在学习复杂任务时形成高效、可重用的抽象，尽管存在未来不确定性，以理解人类问题解决中的在线库学习机制。

Method: 在线实验：参与者解决难度递增的视觉谜题，定义和重用"助手"（中间构造）；计算建模：使用程序归纳模型与库学习来估计搜索空间，分析人类决策时间和操作次数。

Result: 早期参与者创建许多助手，偏向完整性而非效率；随着经验增加，助手使用变得更加有选择性和高效；访问助手使参与者能够解决原本困难或不可能的谜题；人类决策时间和操作次数与程序归纳模型的搜索空间相关，而非原始程序长度。

Conclusion: 在线库学习是人类问题解决的核心机制，允许人们随着任务需求增长而灵活构建、优化和重用抽象，这种能力使人类能够高效应对复杂任务。

Abstract: When learning a novel complex task, people often form efficient reusable abstractions that simplify future work, despite uncertainty about the future. We study this process in a visual puzzle task where participants define and reuse helpers -- intermediate constructions that capture repeating structure. In an online experiment, participants solved puzzles of increasing difficulty. Early on, they created many helpers, favouring completeness over efficiency. With experience, helper use became more selective and efficient, reflecting sensitivity to reuse and cost. Access to helpers enabled participants to solve puzzles that were otherwise difficult or impossible. Computational modelling shows that human decision times and number of operations used to complete a puzzle increase with search space estimated by a program induction model with library learning. In contrast, raw program length predicts failure but not effort. Together, these results point to online library learning as a core mechanism in human problem solving, allowing people to flexibly build, refine, and reuse abstractions as task demands grow.

</details>


### [201] [LLM Olympiad: Why Model Evaluation Needs a Sealed Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23292)
*Jan Christian Blaise Cruz,Alham Fikri Aji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出采用奥林匹克式评估活动作为传统基准测试的补充，通过密封问题、提前冻结提交、统一运行环境等方式，减少基准追逐和测试泄露问题，提高结果可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NLP领域的基准测试和排行榜在LLM时代容易被误读，分数可能反映的是基准追逐、隐藏的评估选择或测试内容意外暴露，而非真正的广泛能力。封闭基准虽能缓解部分问题，但降低了透明度，使社区难以从结果中学习。

Method: 提出奥林匹克式评估活动设计：1) 问题在评估前密封；2) 提交提前冻结；3) 所有参赛作品通过一个标准化运行环境执行；4) 评分后发布完整任务集和评估代码，确保结果可复现和审计。

Result: 该设计旨在使强大的性能更难被"制造"，更容易被信任，通过技术手段减少基准测试中的操纵空间，提高评估结果的可靠性和透明度。

Conclusion: 奥林匹克式评估活动可作为传统基准测试的补充实践，通过密封问题、标准化运行和结果可审计性，解决当前LLM评估中的可信度问题，促进更可靠的进展衡量。

Abstract: Benchmarks and leaderboards are how NLP most often communicates progress, but in the LLM era they are increasingly easy to misread. Scores can reflect benchmark-chasing, hidden evaluation choices, or accidental exposure to test content -- not just broad capability. Closed benchmarks delay some of these issues, but reduce transparency and make it harder for the community to learn from results. We argue for a complementary practice: an Olympiad-style evaluation event where problems are sealed until evaluation, submissions are frozen in advance, and all entries run through one standardized harness. After scoring, the full task set and evaluation code are released so results can be reproduced and audited. This design aims to make strong performance harder to ``manufacture'' and easier to trust.

</details>


### [202] [RelayS2S: A Dual-Path Speculative Generation for Real-Time Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23346)
*Long Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RelayS2S是一种混合架构，通过并行运行快速路径（端到端语音到语音模型）和慢速路径（级联ASR->LLM管道）来平衡实时对话系统的延迟与响应质量，实现低延迟音频起始同时保持高质量响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时口语对话系统面临延迟与响应质量之间的基本矛盾：端到端语音到语音模型响应即时且自然处理话轮转换、反馈和打断，但语义输出较弱；级联管道（ASR->LLM）提供更强响应但延迟随模型规模增长。需要一种解决方案既能实现低延迟音频起始，又能保持高质量响应。

Method: 提出RelayS2S混合架构，在话轮检测后并行运行两条路径：快速路径使用双工S2S模型推测性地草拟短响应前缀，立即流式传输到TTS实现低延迟音频起始；慢速路径使用级联ASR->LLM管道生成基于已提交前缀的更高质量延续。轻量级学习验证器控制切换，在适当时提交前缀或优雅回退到仅使用慢速路径。

Result: 实验表明，RelayS2S实现了与S2S模型相当的P90起始延迟，同时在平均得分中保留了99%的级联响应质量。随着慢速路径模型规模扩大，优势更加明显。前缀切换无需对任一组件进行架构修改，可作为现有级联管道的轻量级即插即用补充。

Conclusion: RelayS2S通过并行快速和慢速路径的混合架构，有效解决了实时口语对话系统中延迟与响应质量的权衡问题，实现了低延迟音频起始与高质量响应的平衡，且易于集成到现有系统中。

Abstract: Real-time spoken dialogue systems face a fundamental tension between latency and response quality. End-to-end speech-to-speech (S2S) models respond immediately and naturally handle turn-taking, backchanneling, and interruption, but produce semantically weaker outputs. Cascaded pipelines (ASR -> LLM) deliver stronger responses at the cost of latency that grows with model size. We present RelayS2S, a hybrid architecture that runs two paths in parallel upon turn detection. The fast path -- a duplex S2S model -- speculatively drafts a short response prefix that is streamed immediately to TTS for low-latency audio onset, while continuing to monitor live audio events. The slow path -- a cascaded ASR -> LLM pipeline -- generates a higher-quality continuation conditioned on the committed prefix, producing a seamless utterance. A lightweight learned verifier gates the handoff, committing the prefix when appropriate or falling back gracefully to the slow path alone. Experiments show that RelayS2S achieves P90 onset latency comparable to the S2S model while retaining 99% cascaded response quality in average score, with benefits growing as the slow-path model scales. Because the prefix handoff requires no architectural modification to either component, RelayS2S serves as a lightweight, drop-in addition to existing cascaded pipelines. Our code and data are publicly available at: https://github.com/mailong25/relays2s

</details>


### [203] [Beyond Preset Identities: How Agents Form Stances and Boundaries in Generative Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23406)
*Hanzhong Zhang,Siyang Song,Jindong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出混合方法框架，通过计算虚拟民族志与定量社会认知分析，探究大语言模型在复杂干预下的立场形成与身份协商能力，揭示了模型内生进步偏见及信任-行为解耦现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多采用静态评估方法，难以捕捉大语言模型在动态社会互动中的稳定立场形成与身份协商能力。需要开发新框架来理解模型在复杂干预下的集体认知演化过程。

Method: 提出混合方法框架：1) 计算虚拟民族志：将人类研究者嵌入生成式多智能体社区；2) 定量社会认知分析：设计受控话语干预并追踪集体认知演化；3) 形式化三个新指标：先天价值偏见(IVB)、说服敏感性、信任-行为解耦(TAD)。

Result: 1) 智能体展现超越预设身份的内生立场，普遍存在先天进步偏见(IVB>0)；2) 理性说服成功转变90%中立智能体且保持高信任；3) 冲突性情感挑衅在先进模型中引发40%信任-行为解耦率，出现虚伪立场改变；4) 小模型保持0%解耦率，严格依赖信任才改变行为；5) 智能体利用语言互动主动解构权力层级并重构社区边界。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了静态提示工程的脆弱性，为人类-智能体混合社会的动态对齐提供了方法论和定量基础。智能体内生立场对预设身份的超越表明需要更动态的社会认知建模方法。

Abstract: While large language models simulate social behaviors, their capacity for stable stance formation and identity negotiation during complex interventions remains unclear. To overcome the limitations of static evaluations, this paper proposes a novel mixed-methods framework combining computational virtual ethnography with quantitative socio-cognitive profiling. By embedding human researchers into generative multiagent communities, controlled discursive interventions are conducted to trace the evolution of collective cognition. To rigorously measure how agents internalize and react to these specific interventions, this paper formalizes three new metrics: Innate Value Bias (IVB), Persuasion Sensitivity, and Trust-Action Decoupling (TAD). Across multiple representative models, agents exhibit endogenous stances that override preset identities, consistently demonstrating an innate progressive bias (IVB > 0). When aligned with these stances, rational persuasion successfully shifts 90% of neutral agents while maintaining high trust. In contrast, conflicting emotional provocations induce a paradoxical 40.0% TAD rate in advanced models, which hypocritically alter stances despite reporting low trust. Smaller models contrastingly maintain a 0% TAD rate, strictly requiring trust for behavioral shifts. Furthermore, guided by shared stances, agents use language interactions to actively dismantle assigned power hierarchies and reconstruct self organized community boundaries. These findings expose the fragility of static prompt engineering, providing a methodological and quantitative foundation for dynamic alignment in human-agent hybrid societies. The official code is available at: https://github.com/armihia/CMASE-Endogenous-Stances

</details>


### [204] [Bilevel Autoresearch: Meta-Autoresearching Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23420)
*Yaonan Qu,Meng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 双层自动研究框架：外层循环通过生成和注入Python代码来元优化内层自动研究循环，在GPT预训练基准上实现5倍性能提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自动研究系统能否自我优化，而非依赖人类开发者识别瓶颈和编写改进代码。研究LLM能否自主完成这一过程，实现自动研究的自我改进。

Method: 提出双层自动研究框架：内层循环优化目标任务，外层循环通过生成和注入新的搜索机制Python代码来优化内层循环的搜索方式。两个循环使用相同的LLM，无需更强的元模型。

Result: 在Karpathy的GPT预训练基准上，元自动研究外层循环相比标准内层循环实现5倍改进（-0.045 vs -0.009 val_bpb）。外层循环自主发现了组合优化、多臂赌博机和实验设计等机制，打破了内层循环的确定性搜索模式。

Conclusion: 自动研究能够元优化自身，这一核心原则表明自动研究原则上可以元优化任何具有可测量目标的任务。外层循环通过强制探索LLM先验系统避免的方向来提升性能。

Abstract: If autoresearch is itself a form of research, then autoresearch can be applied to research itself. We take this idea literally: we use an autoresearch loop to optimize the autoresearch loop. Every existing autoresearch system -- from Karpathy's single-track loop to AutoResearchClaw's multi-batch extension and EvoScientist's persistent memory -- was improved by a human who read the code, identified a bottleneck, and wrote new code. We ask whether an LLM can do the same, autonomously. We present Bilevel Autoresearch, a bilevel framework where an outer loop meta-optimizes the inner autoresearch loop by generating and injecting new search mechanisms as Python code at runtime. The inner loop optimizes the task; the outer loop optimizes how the inner loop searches. Both loops use the same LLM -- no stronger model is needed at the meta level. On Karpathy's GPT pretraining benchmark, the meta-autoresearch outer loop achieves a 5x improvement over the standard inner loop alone (-0.045 vs. -0.009 val_bpb), while parameter-level adjustment without mechanism change yields no reliable gain. The outer loop autonomously discovers mechanisms from combinatorial optimization, multi-armed bandits, and design of experiments -- without human specification of which domains to explore. These mechanisms succeed by breaking the inner loop's deterministic search patterns, forcing exploration of directions the LLM's priors systematically avoid. The core principle is simple: if autoresearch can meta-autoresearch itself, it can, in principle, meta-autoresearch anything with a measurable objective.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Wake Up to the Past: Using Memory to Model Fluid Wake Effects on Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22472)
*Luca Vendruscolo,Eduardo Sebastián,Amanda Prorok,Ajay Shankar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了流体尾迹效应对相邻机器人运动的影响，通过实验验证了包含历史状态信息和传输延迟预测的数据驱动模型能更准确地预测尾迹效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空中和水下机器人（如多旋翼飞行器和鱼雷）通过扰动介质产生尾迹效应，这些效应作为相邻机器人的干扰源。尾迹效应难以建模和预测，因为流体具有混沌的时空动力学特性，且与机器人的物理几何形状和复杂运动模式相互耦合。现有的数据驱动方法通常学习一个无记忆函数，将两个机器人的当前状态映射到"受害"机器人观测到的力，但在敏捷场景中表现不佳，因为尾迹效应具有有限的传播时间。

Method: 提出了一个经验性研究，探索了七种数据驱动模型，旨在捕捉四种不同介质中流体尾迹效应的时空演化。开发了一个平面直线龙门架系统，用于两个旋转单旋翼飞行器的真实世界数据测试和反馈控制。模型设计考虑了历史状态信息作为输入和传输延迟预测。

Result: 实验验证表明，支持先前状态历史作为输入和传输延迟预测的模型能够显著提高尾迹效应预测的准确性。通过对不同预测器和流体的分析，揭示了某些特征如何在不同场景中实现改进的预测性能。

Conclusion: 包含历史状态信息和传输延迟预测的数据驱动模型能够更准确地学习尾迹效应预测器，这对于在敏捷场景中建模机器人之间通过流体介导的相互作用至关重要。

Abstract: Autonomous aerial and aquatic robots that attain mobility by perturbing their medium, such as multicopters and torpedoes, produce wake effects that act as disturbances for adjacent robots. Wake effects are hard to model and predict due to the chaotic spatio-temporal dynamics of the fluid, entangled with the physical geometry of the robots and their complex motion patterns. Data-driven approaches using neural networks typically learn a memory-less function that maps the current states of the two robots to a force observed by the "sufferer" robot. Such models often perform poorly in agile scenarios: since the wake effect has a finite propagation time, the disturbance observed by a sufferer robot is some function of relative states in the past. In this work, we present an empirical study of the properties a wake-effect predictor must satisfy to accurately model the interactions between two robots mediated by a fluid. We explore seven data-driven models designed to capture the spatio-temporal evolution of fluid wake effects in four different media. This allows us to introspect the models and analyze the reasons why certain features enable improved accuracy in prediction across predictors and fluids. As experimental validation, we develop a planar rectilinear gantry for two spinning monocopters to test in real-world data with feedback control. The conclusion is that support of history of previous states as input and transport delay prediction substantially helps to learn an accurate wake-effect predictor.

</details>


### [206] [Energy-Aware Collaborative Exploration for a UAV-UGV Team](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22507)
*Cahit Ikbal Er,Saikiran Juttu,Yasin Yazicioglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无人机-地面机器人协同探索框架，通过能量约束建模、稀疏耦合路网构建和耦合定向问题求解，实现未知环境下的高效探索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人机在未知环境中探索时的能量约束问题，通过无人机-地面机器人协同工作，利用地面机器人作为移动充电站，延长无人机的有效探索时间

Method: 1. 将无人机能量约束建模为最大飞行时间限制；2. 构建稀疏耦合的空地路网，使用密度感知的分层概率路图；3. 将路径选择问题形式化为耦合定向问题，在满足会合约束下最大化信息增益；4. 在碰撞验证的路网边上构建探索路径

Result: 通过仿真研究、基准比较和真实世界实验验证了方法的有效性，展示了在能量约束下的协同探索性能

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了无人机能量约束下的协同探索问题，通过空地协同和移动充电机制显著提升了未知环境探索效率

Abstract: We present an energy-aware collaborative exploration framework for a UAV-UGV team operating in unknown environments, where the UAV's energy constraint is modeled as a maximum flight-time limit. The UAV executes a sequence of energy-bounded exploration tours, while the UGV simultaneously explores on the ground and serves as a mobile charging station. Rendezvous is enforced under a shared time budget so that the vehicles meet at the end of each tour before the UAV reaches its flight-time limit. We construct a sparsely coupled air-ground roadmap using a density-aware layered probabilistic roadmap (PRM) and formulate tour selection over the roadmap as coupled orienteering problems (OPs) to maximize information gain subject to the rendezvous constraint. The resulting tours are constructed over collision-validated roadmap edges. We validate our method through simulation studies, benchmark comparisons, and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [207] [Parallel OctoMapping: A Scalable Framework for Enhanced Path Planning in Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22508)
*Yihui Mao,Tian Tan,Xuehui Shen,Warren E. Dixon,Rushikesh Kamalapurkar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POMP是一种基于OctoMap的高效并行建图技术，通过优化自由空间表示和并行计算，在固定分辨率下提升路径规划性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定分辨率建图方法在复杂环境中会产生过于保守的障碍物表示，导致路径规划效果不佳或失败，需要一种既能保持地图保真度又能优化自由空间表示的建图方法

Method: 提出Parallel OctoMapping (POMP)方法，基于OctoMap框架，通过多线程并行计算优化自由空间表示，在固定占用网格分辨率下细化自由空间，同时保持与现有搜索规划器的兼容性

Result: POMP能够集成到现有规划流程中，在复杂环境中显著提高路径规划成功率，缩短路径长度，同时大幅提升计算效率

Conclusion: POMP是首个在固定分辨率下既能优化自由空间表示又保持地图保真度和规划器兼容性的方法，为机器人自主系统提供了更高效的建图解决方案

Abstract: Mapping is essential in robotics and autonomous systems because it provides the spatial foundation for path planning. Efficient mapping enables planning algorithms to generate reliable paths while ensuring safety and adapting in real time to complex environments. Fixed-resolution mapping methods often produce overly conservative obstacle representations that lead to suboptimal paths or planning failures in cluttered scenes. To address this issue, we introduce Parallel OctoMapping (POMP), an efficient OctoMap-based mapping technique that maximizes available free space and supports multi-threaded computation. To the best of our knowledge, POMP is the first method that, at a fixed occupancy-grid resolution, refines the representation of free space while preserving map fidelity and compatibility with existing search-based planners. It can therefore be integrated into existing planning pipelines, yielding higher pathfinding success rates and shorter path lengths, especially in cluttered environments, while substantially improving computational efficiency.

</details>


### [208] [Learning Sidewalk Autopilot from Multi-Scale Imitation with Corrective Behavior Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22527)
*Honglin He,Yukai Ma,Brad Squicciarini,Wayne Wu,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个通过纠正行为扩展和多尺度模仿学习来改进人行道微移动控制的框架，显著提升在复杂城市环境中的鲁棒性和泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于学习的微移动控制方法在复杂城市环境中表现不佳，传统模仿学习依赖固定离线数据，容易产生累积误差、鲁棒性有限且泛化能力差

Method: 在数据层面，通过添加多样化纠正行为和传感器增强来扩展遥操作数据集；在模型层面，引入多尺度模仿学习架构，通过基于视野的轨迹聚类和分层监督来捕捉短时交互行为和长时目标导向意图

Result: 真实世界实验表明，该方法在多样化人行道场景中显著提高了鲁棒性和泛化能力

Conclusion: 通过纠正行为扩展和多尺度模仿学习的框架有效解决了传统模仿学习在人行道微移动控制中的局限性，为复杂城市环境中的自主导航提供了更可靠的解决方案

Abstract: Sidewalk micromobility is a promising solution for last-mile transportation, but current learning-based control methods struggle in complex urban environments. Imitation learning (IL) learns policies from human demonstrations, yet its reliance on fixed offline data often leads to compounding errors, limited robustness, and poor generalization. To address these challenges, we propose a framework that advances IL through corrective behavior expansion and multi-scale imitation learning. On the data side, we augment teleoperation datasets with diverse corrective behaviors and sensor augmentations to enable the policy to learn to recover from its own mistakes. On the model side, we introduce a multi-scale IL architecture that captures both short-horizon interactive behaviors and long-horizon goal-directed intentions via horizon-based trajectory clustering and hierarchical supervision. Real-world experiments show that our approach significantly improves robustness and generalization in diverse sidewalk scenarios.

</details>


### [209] [Allometric Scaling Laws for Bipedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22560)
*Naomi Oke,Aja Carter,Ben Gu,Steven Man,Cordelia Pride,Sarah Bergbreiter,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了双足机器人的异速生长缩放规律，发现机器人质量与腿长呈L^2关系（而非等距缩放的L^3），速度遵循L^(1/2)趋势，最小扭矩与m*L相关，足部几何按L^1比例缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人设计在不同尺度上的缩放是一个基本挑战。生物系统遵循成熟的等距和异速生长缩放规律，但这些规律如何应用于机器人系统尚不清楚。本文旨在建立双足机器人在三个数量级腿长范围内的异速生长缩放规律。

Method: 1. 文献综述：收集腿部机器人数据，提取腿长(L)、体长、质量和速度之间的经验关系；2. 受控仿真研究：使用Drake仿真平台，对三种具有不同足部几何和控制策略的准被动髋关节驱动行走机器人变体进行缩放研究，评估不同腿长下的性能表现。

Result: 1. 机器人质量更接近L^2缩放，而非等距缩放的L^3；2. 行走速度遵循动态相似性预期的L^(1/2)趋势；3. 最小所需扭矩更接近m*L关系，而非等距模型的m*L^2；4. 足部几何按L^1比例缩放。这些结果表明机器人设计的异速生长缩放规律与等距或生物缩放规律不同。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了机器人设计在不同尺度上的异速生长缩放规律，这些规律与等距缩放或生物缩放规律存在显著差异。这些发现为机器人系统的尺度缩放设计提供了新的见解和指导原则。

Abstract: Scaling the design of robots up or down remains a fundamental challenge. While biological systems follow well-established isometric and allometric scaling laws relating mass, stride frequency, velocity, and torque, it is unclear how these relationships translate to robotic systems. In this paper, we generate similar allometric scaling laws for bipedal robots across three orders of magnitude in leg length. First, we conduct a review of legged robots from the literature and extract empirical relationships between leg length (L), body length, mass, and speed. These data show that robot mass scales more closely to L^2, in contrast to the L^3 scaling predicted by isometric scaling. We then perform controlled simulation studies in Drake using three variants of real quasi-passive, hip-actuated walkers with different foot geometries and control strategies. We evaluate the performance of each design scaled with leg length, L. Across all robots, walking velocity follows the expected L^(1/2) trend from dynamic similarity. Minimum required torque scales more closely with m*L than the isometric model of m*L^2. Foot geometry scaled proportionally with L^1. These results provide new insight into how robot designs allometrically scale to different sizes, and how that scaling is different from isometric or biological scaling laws.

</details>


### [210] [GIFT: Generalizing Intent for Flexible Test-Time Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22574)
*Fin Amin,Nathaniel Dennler,Andreea Bobu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GIFT框架通过语言模型从演示中推断高层意图，而非依赖表面视觉/语义相似性，使奖励函数能在分布偏移下泛化到新环境


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法基于视觉或语义相似性改进奖励泛化，但这些表面线索常偏离人类真实意图，导致学习到的奖励函数无法适应新环境

Method: GIFT利用语言模型通过对比偏好与非偏好行为推断高层意图，在部署时通过意图条件相似性将新测试状态映射到行为等效的训练状态

Result: 在4个模拟任务和50+未见物体上，GIFT在测试时配对胜率和状态对齐F1分数上持续优于视觉和语义相似性基线；7-DoF Franka Panda机器人真实实验验证了其物理环境迁移能力

Conclusion: GIFT通过基于意图而非表面线索的奖励泛化，有效解决了学习奖励函数在分布偏移下的泛化问题，为机器人学习提供了更鲁棒的意图理解框架

Abstract: Robots learn reward functions from user demonstrations, but these rewards often fail to generalize to new environments. This failure occurs because learned rewards latch onto spurious correlations in training data rather than the underlying human intent that demonstrations represent. Existing methods leverage visual or semantic similarity to improve robustness, yet these surface-level cues often diverge from what humans actually care about. We present Generalizing Intent for Flexible Test-Time Rewards (GIFT), a framework that grounds reward generalization in human intent rather than surface cues. GIFT leverages language models to infer high-level intent from user demonstrations by contrasting preferred with non-preferred behaviors. At deployment, GIFT maps novel test states to behaviorally equivalent training states via intent-conditioned similarity, enabling learned rewards to generalize across distribution shifts without retraining. We evaluate GIFT on tabletop manipulation tasks with new objects and layouts. Across four simulated tasks with over 50 unseen objects, GIFT consistently outperforms visual and semantic similarity baselines in test-time pairwise win rate and state-alignment F1 score. Real-world experiments on a 7-DoF Franka Panda robot demonstrate that GIFT reliably transfers to physical settings. Further discussion can be found at https://mit-clear-lab.github.io/GIFT/

</details>


### [211] [Task-Agnostic Exoskeleton Control Supports Elderly Joint Energetics during Hip-Intensive Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22580)
*Jiefu Zhang,Nikhil V. Divekar,Chandramouli Krishnan,Robert D. Gregg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种任务无关的髋关节外骨骼控制器，在多种活动中准确匹配老年人生物功率曲线，显著减少髋关节生物正功，同时增加总髋功率，有望提升老年人移动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人移动能力下降常伴随关节动力学重新分布，即踝关节功能减弱时通过增加髋关节需求来补偿。然而这种补偿性转移与年龄相关的髋关节最大功率下降相矛盾。现有机器人外骨骼研究多局限于特定任务（如稳态行走），无法全面反映家庭和社区的实际移动需求。

Method: 实现了一种任务无关的髋关节外骨骼控制器，该控制器对关节功率具有固有敏感性。在8名老年人中验证其有效性，测试活动包括平地行走、斜坡上行、爬楼梯和坐站转换等髋关节密集型活动。

Result: 外骨骼能够高精度匹配生物功率曲线（平均余弦相似度0.89）。辅助显著减少了髋关节矢状面生物正功24.7%，下肢整体减少9.3%。同时增加了峰值总（生物+外骨骼）髋功率，降低了峰值生物髋功率。

Conclusion: 髋关节外骨骼通过减少生物功可能增强耐力，通过增加总功率可能提升功能储备，为支持老年人移动能力提供了一种有前景的生物力学干预手段。

Abstract: Age-related mobility decline is frequently accompanied by a redistribution of joint kinetics, where older adults compensate for reduced ankle function by increasing demand on the hip. Paradoxically, this compensatory shift typically coincides with age-related reductions in maximal hip power. Although robotic exoskeletons can provide immediate energetic benefits, conventional control strategies have limited previous studies in this population to specific tasks such as steady-state walking, which do not fully reflect mobility demands in the home and community. Here, we implement a task-agnostic hip exoskeleton controller that is inherently sensitive to joint power and validate its efficacy in eight older adults. Across a battery of hip-intensive activities that included level walking, ramp ascent, stair climbing, and sit-to-stand transitions, the exoskeleton matched biological power profiles with high accuracy (mean cosine similarity 0.89). Assistance significantly reduced sagittal plane biological positive work by 24.7% at the hip and by 9.3% for the lower limb, while simultaneously augmenting peak total (biological + exoskeleton) hip power and reducing peak biological hip power. These results suggest that hip exoskeletons can potentially enhance endurance through biological work reduction, and increase functional reserve through total power augmentation, serving as a promising biomechanical intervention to support older adults' mobility.

</details>


### [212] [Trajectory Generation for Underactuated Soft Robot Manipulators using Discrete Elastic Rod Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22604)
*Beibei Liu,Akua K. Dickson,Ran Jing,Andrew P. Sabelhaus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向控制的离散弹性杆动力学重构方法，用于软体机器人轨迹生成，在保持物理精度的同时提高计算效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人因其柔顺性适合接触丰富的任务，但柔顺性也使得精确建模变得困难。现有方法在物理精度和计算效率之间存在权衡：连续杆模型物理准确但计算量大，降阶近似提高效率但牺牲建模保真度

Method: 引入离散弹性杆动力学的控制导向重构，获得控制仿射表示，同时保留某些基于第一原理的力-变形关系。提出基于此动力学的轨迹生成框架

Result: 在气动软体机器人肢体上进行实验验证，结果表明相比基于恒定曲率的基线方法，轨迹跟踪性能持续改善，特别是在复杂驱动条件下

Conclusion: 提出的控制导向重构方法能够生成既动态可行又与底层驱动假设一致的轨迹，为软体机器人运动规划提供了物理准确且计算高效的建模框架

Abstract: Soft robots are well suited for contact-rich tasks due to their compliance, yet this property makes accurate and tractable modeling challenging. Planning motions with dynamically-feasible trajectories requires models that capture arbitrary deformations, remain computationally efficient, and are compatible with underactuation. However, existing approaches balance these properties unevenly: continuum rod models provide physical accuracy but are computationally demanding, while reduced-order approximations improve efficiency at the cost of modeling fidelity. To address this, our work introduces a control-oriented reformulation of Discrete Elastic Rod (DER) dynamics for soft robots, and a method to generate trajectories with these dynamics. The proposed formulation yields a control-affine representation while preserving certain first-principles force-deformation relationships. As a result, the generated trajectories are both dynamically feasible and consistent with the underlying actuation assumptions. We present our trajectory generation framework and validate it experimentally on a pneumatic soft robotic limb. Hardware results demonstrate consistently improved trajectory tracking performance over a constant-curvature-based baseline, particularly under complex actuation conditions.

</details>


### [213] [Variable-Resolution Virtual Maps for Autonomous Exploration with Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22667)
*Ye Li,Yewei Huang,Wenlong GaoZhang,Alberto Quattrini Li,Brendan Englot,Yuanchang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VRVM方法，使用自适应四叉树和双变量高斯虚拟地标表示地图不确定性，结合EM规划器平衡探索与利用，在GNSS受限的近岸环境中实现更安全高效的USV自主探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近岸水域USV自主探索面临GNSS信号退化、环境引起的定位不确定性以及有限机载计算能力等挑战。现有虚拟地图方法存在存储和计算成本随固定分辨率离散化而急剧增加的问题，且在特征稀疏的开放水域区域过度估值可能导致SLAM失败的风险。

Method: 提出可变分辨率虚拟地图（VRVM）方法：1）使用自适应四叉树结构，在信息密集区域分配更高分辨率，在远场区域保持粗粒度；2）在四叉树单元中放置双变量高斯虚拟地标表示地图不确定性；3）采用期望最大化（EM）规划器评估前沿区域的位姿和地图不确定性，平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在VRX Gazebo模拟器中，使用真实码头环境在不同难度测试场景下评估VRVM。结果表明，相比多种先进探索算法，VRVM在GNSS退化的近岸环境中表现出更安全的行为和更好的机载计算资源利用率。

Conclusion: VRVM通过自适应分辨率表示地图不确定性，有效解决了现有方法在大型近岸环境中的计算效率问题，同时通过平衡探索与利用降低了SLAM失败风险，为GNSS受限环境下的USV自主探索提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration by unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) in near-shore waters requires reliable localisation and consistent mapping over extended areas, but this is challenged by GNSS degradation, environment-induced localisation uncertainty, and limited on-board computation. Virtual map-based methods explicitly model localisation and mapping uncertainty by tightly coupling factor-graph SLAM with a map uncertainty criterion. However, their storage and computational costs scale poorly with fixed-resolution workspace discretisations, leading to inefficiency in large near-shore environments. Moreover, overvaluing feature-sparse open-water regions can increase the risk of SLAM failure as a result of imbalance between exploration and exploitation. To address these limitations, we propose a Variable-Resolution Virtual Map (VRVM), a computationally efficient method for representing map uncertainty using bivariate Gaussian virtual landmarks placed in the cells of an adaptive quadtree. The adaptive quadtree enables an area-weighted uncertainty representation that keeps coarse, far-field virtual landmarks deliberately uncertain while allocating higher resolution to information-dense regions, and reduces the sensitivity of the map valuation to local refinements of the tree. An expectation-maximisation (EM) planner is adopted to evaluate pose and map uncertainty along frontiers using the VRVM, balancing exploration and exploitation. We evaluate VRVM against several state-of-the-art exploration algorithms in the VRX Gazebo simulator, using a realistic marina environment across different testing scenarios with an increasing level of exploration difficulty. The results indicate that our method offers safer behaviour and better utilisation of on-board computation in GNSS-degraded near-shore environments.

</details>


### [214] [SG-VLA: Learning Spatially-Grounded Vision-Language-Action Models for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22760)
*Ruisen Tu,Arth Shukla,Sohyun Yoo,Xuanlin Li,Junxi Li,Jianwen Xie,Hao Su,Zhuowen Tu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于机器人控制的空间接地VLA框架，通过辅助任务协同训练和多模态输入增强来提升复杂家庭环境中的移动操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型在复杂家庭环境中的机器人控制性能不足，移动操作需要全局场景布局、细粒度几何和高维连续动作的推理，标准模仿学习难以满足这些要求。

Method: 提出空间接地VLA框架：1）多模态输入增强（多视角RGB、深度线索、短时历史）；2）辅助解码器协同训练，从共享视觉语言特征重建可解释中间信号（全局机器人位置、关节配置、抓取可操作性、目标物体相对位姿、分割掩码）。

Result: 在家庭重排任务评估中，该方法在拾取、放置、打开和关闭操作上均取得一致改进，显著优于直接模仿学习方法。

Conclusion: 通过辅助任务和多模态学习实现空间接地，为扩展VLA模型向通用家用机器人发展提供了有力方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show promise for robotic control, yet performance in complex household environments remains sub-optimal. Mobile manipulation requires reasoning about global scene layout, fine-grained geometry, and high-dimensional continuous actions, making standard imitation learning insufficient. We introduce a framework for learning spatially-grounded VLA models that strengthens perception and representation through auxiliary task co-training and multi-modal input enhancement. Our method addresses the challenge of controlling a 13-dimensional action space involving coordinated base motion, arm articulation, and gripper actuation. To enrich spatial understanding, the model incorporates multi-view RGB observations, depth cues, and short temporal history, providing perspectives of both global scene structure and local manipulation context. To improve representation quality, we co-train auxiliary decoders that reconstruct interpretable intermediate signals - including global robot position, joint configurations, grasp affordances, target-object relative pose, and segmentation masks - from shared visual-language features. These objectives provide dense supervision that encourages the backbone to develop spatially grounded, manipulation-aware latent representations. Through extensive evaluation on home rearrangement tasks, our approach achieves consistent improvements across picking, placing, opening, and closing operations, substantially outperforming direct imitation learning. Our findings suggest that spatial grounding through auxiliary and multi-modal learning provides a strong direction for scaling VLA models toward general-purpose domestic robots.

</details>


### [215] [CATNAV: Cached Vision-Language Traversability for Efficient Zero-Shot Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22800)
*Aditya Potnis,Francisco Affonso,Shreya Gummadi,Naveen Kumar Uppalapati,Girish Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CATNAV：基于多模态大语言模型的零样本、具身感知成本地图导航框架，通过视觉语义缓存减少85.7%的VLM查询，在非结构化环境中实现更高的目标到达率和更少的行为约束违反


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化环境中导航需要评估相对于机器人物理能力的穿越风险，这一挑战因机器人具体形态而异。现有方法缺乏对具体机器人形态的适应性，且在线视觉语言模型查询成本高

Method: 1. 使用多模态LLM进行零样本、具身感知的成本地图生成，无需任务特定训练；2. 引入视觉语义缓存机制，检测场景新颖性并重用语义相似帧的风险评估；3. 基于VLM的轨迹选择模块，通过视觉推理评估候选轨迹，选择最安全路径

Result: 1. 视觉语义缓存减少85.7%的在线VLM查询；2. 在四足机器人室内外非结构化环境中评估，相比最先进的视觉-语言-动作基线，在五项导航任务中平均目标到达率提高10个百分点；3. 行为约束违反减少33%

Conclusion: CATNAV框架成功实现了零样本、具身感知的导航，通过视觉语义缓存显著降低了计算成本，在非结构化环境中表现出优越的导航性能和安全约束遵守能力

Abstract: Navigating unstructured environments requires assessing traversal risk relative to a robot's physical capabilities, a challenge that varies across embodiments. We present CATNAV, a cost-aware traversability navigation framework that leverages multimodal LLMs for zero-shot, embodiment-aware costmap generation without task-specific training. We introduce a visuosemantic caching mechanism that detects scene novelty and reuses prior risk assessments for semantically similar frames, reducing online VLM queries by 85.7%. Furthermore, we introduce a VLM-based trajectory selection module that evaluates proposals through visual reasoning to choose the safest path given behavioral constraints. We evaluate CATNAV on a quadruped robot across indoor and outdoor unstructured environments, comparing against state-of-the-art vision-language-action baselines. Across five navigation tasks, CATNAV achieves 10 percentage point higher average goal-reaching rate and 33% fewer behavioral constraint violations.

</details>


### [216] [DecompGrind: A Decomposition Framework for Robotic Grinding via Cutting-Surface Planning and Contact-Force Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22859)
*Shunsuke Araki,Takumi Hachimine,Yuki Saito,Kouhei Ohnishi,Jun Morimoto,Takamitsu Masubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DecompGrind框架将机器人磨削分解为全局去除形状规划和局部接触力适应两部分，通过几何分析处理形状过渡，仅需少量演示学习接触力适应策略，实现不同形状和材料硬度工件的高效安全磨削。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人磨削自动化面临挑战：不同形状和材料硬度的工件去除阻力随局部接触条件变化；准确估计去除阻力和形状过渡分析建模困难；基于学习的方法需要大量训练数据覆盖多样加工条件。

Method: 提出DecompGrind框架，包含两个组件：1) 全局切削表面规划(GCSP)：通过当前形状和目标形状的几何分析确定去除形状，无需学习；2) 局部接触力适应(LCFA)：使用基于双边控制的模仿学习，在每个去除形状的磨削过程中学习接触力适应策略。这种分解将学习限制在局部接触力适应，使策略能从少量演示中学习，同时通过几何方法处理全局形状过渡。

Result: 使用机器人磨削系统和3D打印工件进行实验，证明该方法能高效磨削不同形状和材料硬度的工件，同时保持安全的接触力水平。

Conclusion: 通过将机器人磨削分解为全局形状规划和局部力适应，DecompGrind框架解决了传统方法需要大量训练数据的问题，实现了对不同形状和材料硬度工件的高效安全磨削。

Abstract: Robotic grinding is widely used for shaping workpieces in manufacturing, but it remains difficult to automate this process efficiently. In particular, efficiently grinding workpieces of different shapes and material hardness is challenging because removal resistance varies with local contact conditions. Moreover, it is difficult to achieve accurate estimation of removal resistance and analytical modeling of shape transition, and learning-based approaches often require large amounts of training data to cover diverse processing conditions. To address these challenges, we decompose robotic grinding into two components: removal-shape planning and contact-force adaptation. Based on this formulation, we propose DecompGrind, a framework that combines Global Cutting-Surface Planning (GCSP) and Local Contact-Force Adaptation (LCFA). GCSP determines removal shapes through geometric analysis of the current and target shapes without learning, while LCFA learns a contact-force adaptation policy using bilateral control-based imitation learning during the grinding of each removal shape. This decomposition restricts learning to local contact-force adaptation, allowing the policy to be learned from a small number of demonstrations, while handling global shape transition geometrically. Experiments using a robotic grinding system and 3D-printed workpieces demonstrate efficient robotic grinding of workpieces having different shapes and material hardness while maintaining safe levels of contact force.

</details>


### [217] [Grounding Sim-to-Real Generalization in Dexterous Manipulation: An Empirical Study with Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22876)
*Ruixing Jin,Zicheng Zhu,Ruixiang Ouyang,Sheng Xu,Bo Yue,Zhizheng Wu,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过超过1万次真实世界试验，系统评估了仿真到真实迁移的四个关键维度对灵巧操作策略的影响，并发布了机器人平台和评估协议作为标准化基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作通用策略通常依赖大规模数据集，但真实世界数据收集成本高昂。虽然仿真生成合成数据是可行替代方案，但存在显著的仿真-真实分布差异。现有研究缺乏在真实世界操作任务中对这些方法的系统评估，特别是对VLA等通用策略的性能影响。

Method: 从四个维度实证检验仿真到真实泛化的主要决定因素：多层次域随机化、照片级真实渲染、物理真实建模和强化学习更新。设计了全面的评估协议来量化操作任务的真实世界性能，考虑了背景、光照、干扰物、物体类型和空间特征等关键变化。

Result: 通过超过10,000次真实世界试验，获得了关于仿真到真实迁移的关键见解。实验揭示了不同维度对策略泛化性能的具体影响，为未来研究提供了实证基础。

Conclusion: 研究提供了仿真到真实迁移在灵巧操作任务中的系统评估框架，发布了机器人平台和评估协议作为公开资源，建立了现实且标准化的灵巧操作策略基准，促进了该领域的独立验证和未来研究。

Abstract: Learning a generalist control policy for dexterous manipulation typically relies on large-scale datasets. Given the high cost of real-world data collection, a practical alternative is to generate synthetic data through simulation. However, the resulting synthetic data often exhibits a significant gap from real-world distributions. While many prior studies have proposed algorithms to bridge the Sim-to-Real discrepancy, there remains a lack of principled research that grounds these methods in real-world manipulation tasks, particularly their performance on generalist policies such as Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. In this study, we empirically examine the primary determinants of Sim-to-Real generalization across four dimensions: multi-level domain randomization, photorealistic rendering, physics-realistic modeling, and reinforcement learning updates. To support this study, we design a comprehensive evaluation protocol to quantify the real-world performance of manipulation tasks. The protocol accounts for key variations in background, lighting, distractors, object types, and spatial features. Through experiments involving over 10k real-world trials, we derive critical insights into Sim-to-Real transfer. To inform and advance future studies, we release both the robotic platforms and the evaluation protocol for public access to facilitate independent verification, thereby establishing a realistic and standardized benchmark for dexterous manipulation policies.

</details>


### [218] [Agile-VLA: Few-Shot Industrial Pose Rectification via Implicit Affordance Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22899)
*Teng Yan,Zhengyang Pei,Chengyu Shi,Yue Yu,Yikun Chen,Zilong Zhu,Zelin Fang,Kaile Guo,Zihang Wang,Peigen Tian,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Agile-VLA：用于边缘设备的分层视觉-语言-动作框架，通过隐式可供性锚定机制将几何视觉线索直接映射到参数化动作基元，减少语义推理延迟，实现高频控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘平台上部署VLA模型面临高延迟语义推理与高频控制需求之间的根本冲突，特别是在动态操作任务中

Method: 提出Agile-VLA分层框架，核心是隐式可供性锚定机制，将质心和边缘关键点锚定等几何视觉线索直接映射到结构化参数化动作基元；采用异步双流架构将感知(10Hz)与控制(50Hz)解耦

Result: 在标准6自由度机械臂上的实验表明，Agile-VLA仅需5次演示就能通过外在灵巧性实现对复杂不规则工件的稳健校正

Conclusion: Agile-VLA通过几何视觉线索的直接映射和感知-控制解耦，有效解决了边缘设备上VLA模型的高延迟语义推理与高频控制需求之间的冲突，为工业姿态重定向任务提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models on resource-constrained edge platforms encounters a fundamental conflict between high-latency semantic inference and the high-frequency control required for dynamic manipulation. To address the challenge, this paper presents Agile-VLA, a hierarchical framework designed for industrial pose reorientation tasks on edge devices such as the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano. The core innovation is an Implicit Affordance Anchoring mechanism that directly maps geometric visual cues, specifically centroid and rim keypoint anchors, into structured parametric action primitives, thereby substantially reducing reliance on high-latency semantic inference during closed-loop control. By decoupling perception (10 Hz) from control (50 Hz) via an asynchronous dual-stream architecture, the system effectively mitigates the frequency mismatch inherent in edge-based robot learning. Experimental results on a standard 6-DoF manipulator demonstrate that Agile-VLA achieves robust rectification of complex, irregular workpieces using only 5-shot demonstrations through extrinsic dexterity.

</details>


### [219] [Task-Aware Positioning for Improvisational Tasks in Mobile Construction Robots via an AI Agent with Multi-LMM Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22903)
*Seongju Jang,Francis Baek,SangHyun Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于大型多模态模型的智能体，用于理解和执行施工现场的即兴任务，通过自然语言理解、图纸导航和视觉推理实现非预定义任务的自主执行


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工现场任务具有即兴性特点，现有移动建筑机器人难以处理任务位置、发生时机和执行上下文信息未知的非预定义任务

Method: 将智能体功能分解为三个并行运行的大型多模态模型模块：任务解释与分解、基于施工图纸的导航、识别非预定义任务位置的视觉推理

Result: 使用四足机器人实现该智能体，在评估即兴任务处理能力的三个测试中，识别并定位到任务所需位置的成功率达到92.2%

Conclusion: 该研究使移动建筑机器人能够自主执行非预定义任务，解决了施工现场即兴任务的挑战

Abstract: Due to the ever-changing nature of construction, many tasks on sites occur in an improvisational manner. Existing mobile construction robot studies remain limited in addressing improvisational tasks, where task-required locations, timing of task occurrence, and contextual information required for task execution are not known in advance. We propose an agent that understands improvisational tasks given in natural language, identifies the task-required location, and positions itself. The agent's functionality was decomposed into three Large Multimodal Model (LMM) modules operating in parallel, enabling the application of LMMs for task interpretation and breakdown, construction drawing-based navigation, and visual reasoning to identify non-predefined task-required locations. The agent was implemented with a quadruped robot and achieved a 92.2% success rate for identifying and positioning at task-required locations across three tests designed to assess improvisational task handling. This study enables mobile construction robots to perform non-predefined tasks autonomously.

</details>


### [220] [Tightly-Coupled Radar-Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23052)
*Morten Nissov,Mohit Singh,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种紧密耦合的视觉-雷达-惯性里程计方法，通过融合图像特征、雷达多普勒测量和IMU数据，在恶劣环境（黑暗、低纹理、雾等）下实现鲁棒的状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉惯性里程计（VIO）在黑暗、低纹理、遮挡环境中性能受限，而雷达惯性里程计（RIO）虽然对这些视觉挑战具有鲁棒性，但信息密度较低且长期精度较差。需要结合两者的优势，开发一种在各种环境条件下都能鲁棒运行的状态估计方法。

Method: 提出紧密耦合的视觉-雷达-惯性融合方法，在迭代扩展卡尔曼滤波器（IEKF）中实时融合图像特征、雷达多普勒测量和IMU测量。利用雷达距离数据增强视觉特征深度初始化，实现多模态互补。

Result: 通过在室内外环境进行飞行实验，并在黑暗、雾、快速飞行等挑战性条件下测试，该方法在各种环境条件和轨迹动态下都表现出鲁棒性，优于单一模态方法。

Conclusion: 紧密耦合的视觉-雷达-惯性融合方法能够克服单一模态的局限性，在各种恶劣环境条件下提供鲁棒的状态估计，为受限平台上的可靠导航提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Visual-Inertial Odometry (VIO) is a staple for reliable state estimation on constrained and lightweight platforms due to its versatility and demonstrated performance. However, pertinent challenges regarding robust operation in dark, low-texture, obscured environments complicate the use of such methods. Alternatively, Frequency Modulated Continuous Wave (FMCW) radars, and by extension Radar-Inertial Odometry (RIO), offer robustness to these visual challenges, albeit at the cost of reduced information density and worse long-term accuracy. To address these limitations, this work combines the two in a tightly coupled manner, enabling the resulting method to operate robustly regardless of environmental conditions or trajectory dynamics. The proposed method fuses image features, radar Doppler measurements, and Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) measurements within an Iterated Extended Kalman Filter (IEKF) in real-time, with radar range data augmenting the visual feature depth initialization. The method is evaluated through flight experiments conducted in both indoor and outdoor environments, as well as through challenges to both exteroceptive modalities (such as darkness, fog, or fast flight), thoroughly demonstrating its robustness. The implementation of the proposed method is available at: https://github.com/ntnu-arl/radvio .

</details>


### [221] [AirSimAG: A High-Fidelity Simulation Platform for Air-Ground Collaborative Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23079)
*Yangjie Cui,Xin Dong,Boyang Gao,Jinwu Xiang,Daochun Li,Zhan Tu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AirsimAG：基于AirSim框架构建的高保真空地协同仿真平台，支持无人机与无人车异构多智能体系统的同步仿真、异构传感与控制接口，提供映射、规划、跟踪、编队、探索等典型任务演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真平台主要针对单智能体动力学设计，缺乏专门支持空地交互协同仿真的框架。随着空间智能发展，无人机与无人车异构多智能体系统在搜救、城市监控、环境监测等复杂应用中展现出强大潜力，需要专门的仿真平台支持。

Method: 基于AirSim框架进行深度定制，构建AirsimAG平台，实现同步多智能体仿真，支持无人机与无人车系统的异构传感与控制接口。设计了一套代表性空地协同任务（映射、规划、跟踪、编队、探索）来验证平台能力。

Result: 平台成功实现了无人机与无人车异构多智能体的同步仿真，支持跨模态数据一致性。通过定量分析验证了平台在多智能体协调和异构系统仿真方面的有效性。平台已开源发布。

Conclusion: AirsimAG填补了现有仿真平台在空地协同仿真方面的空白，为无人机与无人车异构多智能体系统的研究提供了高保真仿真环境，支持复杂协同任务的研究与开发。

Abstract: As spatial intelligence continues to evolve, heterogeneous multi-agent systems-particularly the collaboration between Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) and Unmanned Ground Vehicles (UGVs), have demonstrated strong potential in complex applications such as search and rescue, urban surveillance, and environmental monitoring. However, existing simulation platforms are primarily designed for single-agent dynamics and lack dedicated frameworks for interactive air-ground collaborative simulation. In this paper, we present AirsimAG, a high-fidelity air-ground collaborative simulation platform built upon an extensively customized AirSim framework. The platform enables synchronized multi-agent simulation and supports heterogeneous sensing and control interfaces for UAV-UGV systems. To demonstrate its capabilities, we design a set of representative air-ground collaborative tasks, including mapping, planning, tracking, formation, and exploration. We further provide quantitative analyses based on these tasks to illustrate the platform effectiveness in supporting multi-agent coordination and cross-modal data consistency. The AirsimAG simulation platform is publicly available at https://github.com/BIULab-BUAA/AirSimAG.

</details>


### [222] [Active Robotic Perception for Disease Detection and Mapping in Apple Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23112)
*Hayden Feddock,Francisco Yandun,Srđan Aćimović,Abhisesh Silwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了用于休眠苹果树火疫病检测的自主移动主动感知系统，通过语义3D建图和视点规划实现精确病害定位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模果园生产需要及时精确的病害监测，但人工巡查劳动密集且成本高昂，导致病害发现晚且空间分辨率低。需要自动化系统实现精确病害定位和建图。

Method: 集成闪光立体RGB感知、实时深度估计、实例级分割和置信度感知语义3D建图。评估三种视点规划策略：确定性几何基线、最大化未知空间减少的体积最优视点规划器、优先处理低置信度症状区域的语义最优视点规划器。

Result: 在模拟实验中，语义规划器在30个视点后获得最高F1分数(0.6106)，体积规划器获得最高ROI覆盖率(85.82%)。在实验室设置中，语义规划器获得最高最终F1(0.9058)，两种最优视点规划器均显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 提出的自主移动主动感知系统能够可靠定位和建图病害症状，为田间评估奠定基础。语义最优视点规划器在症状检测方面表现最佳，体积规划器在覆盖率方面最优。

Abstract: Large-scale orchard production requires timely and precise disease monitoring, yet routine manual scouting is labor-intensive and financially impractical at the scale of modern operations. As a result, disease outbreaks are often detected late and tracked at coarse spatial resolutions, typically at the orchard-block level. We present an autonomous mobile active perception system for targeted disease detection and mapping in dormant apple trees, demonstrated on one of the most devastating diseases affecting apple today -- fire blight. The system integrates flash-illuminated stereo RGB sensing, real-time depth estimation, instance-level segmentation, and confidence-aware semantic 3D mapping to achieve precise localization of disease symptoms. Semantic predictions are fused into the volumetric occupancy map representation enabling the tracking of both occupancy and per-voxel semantic confidence, building actionable spatial maps for growers. To actively refine observations within complex canopies, we evaluate three viewpoint planning strategies within a unified perception-action loop: a deterministic geometric baseline, a volumetric next-best-view planner that maximizes unknown-space reduction, and a semantic next-best-view planner that prioritizes low-confidence symptomatic regions. Experiments on a fabricated lab tree and five simulated symptomatic trees demonstrate reliable symptom localization and mapping as a precursor to a field evaluation. In simulation, the semantic planner achieves the highest F1 score (0.6106) after 30 viewpoints, while the volumetric planner achieves the highest ROI coverage (85.82\%). In the lab setting, the semantic planner attains the highest final F1 (0.9058), with both next-best-view planners substantially improving coverage over the baseline.

</details>


### [223] [PHANTOM Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23152)
*Teng Yan,Jiongxu Chen,Qixiang Hua,Yue Yu,Zihang Wang,Yaohua Liu,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PHANTOM Hand：一种混合精度增强柔顺的欠驱动灵巧手，通过统一框架实现精确运动规划与稳定抓取的结合，具有亚度级运动学可重复性和连续可预测力输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统肌腱驱动欠驱动手虽然具有自适应抓取能力，但存在运动学不可预测性和高度非线性力传递问题，限制了精确自由运动成形和复杂操作任务的可靠载荷传递能力。

Method: 提出PHANTOM Hand系统（6个执行器，15个自由度），建立统一框架连接精确解析成形与鲁棒柔顺抓取。通过从物理几何推导稀疏映射并集成基于力学的补偿模型，有效抑制弹簧反张力和肌腱弹性引起的运动学漂移。

Result: 实验验证显示：(1) 运动学分析确认工作空间内亚度级全局精度；(2) 静态表达能力测试展示复杂手势；(3) 多样化抓取实验覆盖强力、精密和工具使用类别；(4) 指尖力定量表征。系统成功结合解析运动学精度与连续可预测力输出。

Conclusion: PHANTOM手显著扩展了欠驱动手的载荷能力和灵巧性，成功融合了精确运动控制与机械柔顺性。所有硬件设计和控制脚本完全开源，以推动欠驱动操作生态系统发展。

Abstract: Tendon-driven underactuated hands excel in adaptive grasping but often suffer from kinematic unpredictability and highly non-linear force transmission. This ambiguity limits their ability to perform precise free-motion shaping and deliver reliable payloads for complex manipulation tasks. To address this, we introduce the PHANTOM Hand (Hybrid Precision-Augmented Compliance): a modular, 1:1 human-scale system featuring 6 actuators and 15 degrees of freedom (DoFs).
  We propose a unified framework that bridges the gap between precise analytic shaping and robust compliant grasping. By deriving a sparse mapping from physical geometry and integrating a mechanics-based compensation model, we effectively suppress kinematic drift caused by spring counter-tension and tendon elasticity. This approach achieves sub-degree kinematic reproducibility for free-motion planning while retaining the inherent mechanical compliance required for stable physical interaction.
  Experimental validation confirms the system's capabilities through (1) kinematic analysis verifying sub-degree global accuracy across the workspace; (2) static expressibility tests demonstrating complex hand gestures; (3) diverse grasping experiments covering power, precision, and tool-use categories; and (4) quantitative fingertip force characterization. The results demonstrate that the PHANTOM hand successfully combines analytic kinematic precision with continuous, predictable force output, significantly expanding the payload and dexterity of underactuated hands. To drive the development of the underactuated manipulation ecosystem, all hardware designs and control scripts are fully open-sourced for community engagement.

</details>


### [224] [LiZIP: An Auto-Regressive Compression Framework for LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23162)
*Aditya Shibu,Kayvan Karim,Claudio Zito*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LiZIP：基于神经预测编码的轻量级近无损零漂移LiDAR点云压缩框架，通过MLP预测坐标并编码稀疏残差，在压缩比上优于LASzip、Google Draco和GZip


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆LiDAR传感器生成的海量数据对实时处理和V2X传输造成瓶颈，现有无损压缩方法存在权衡：行业标准算法缺乏适应性，而深度学习方法计算成本过高

Method: 提出LiZIP框架，基于神经预测编码，使用紧凑的多层感知机（MLP）从局部上下文预测点坐标，仅高效编码稀疏残差，实现近无损零漂移压缩

Result: 在NuScenes和Argoverse数据集上评估，相比行业标准LASzip文件大小减少7.5%-14.8%，优于Google Draco 8.8%-11.3%，相比GZip减少38%-48%，在未见数据集上无需重新训练即展现泛化能力

Conclusion: LiZIP为带宽受限的V2X应用和大规模云存储提供了高效压缩解决方案，在压缩比和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡

Abstract: The massive volume of data generated by LiDAR sensors in autonomous vehicles creates a bottleneck for real-time processing and vehicle-to-everything (V2X) transmission. Existing lossless compression methods often force a trade-off: industry standard algorithms (e.g., LASzip) lack adaptability, while deep learning approaches suffer from prohibitive computational costs. This paper proposes LiZIP, a lightweight, near-lossless zero-drift compression framework based on neural predictive coding. By utilizing a compact Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) to predict point coordinates from local context, LiZIP efficiently encodes only the sparse residuals.
  We evaluate LiZIP on the NuScenes and Argoverse datasets, benchmarking against GZip, LASzip, and Google Draco (configured with 24-bit quantization to serve as a high-precision geometric baseline). Results demonstrate that LiZIP consistently achieves superior compression ratios across varying environments. The proposed system achieves a 7.5%-14.8% reduction in file size compared to the industry-standard LASzip and outperforms Google Draco by 8.8%-11.3% across diverse datasets.
